## Overview

Dr. David S. Liebeskind is a neurologist in Los Angeles, California and is affiliated with multiple hospitals in the area, including [UCLA Medical Center](https://health.usnews.com/best-hospitals/area/ca/ucla-medical-center-6931755) and [UCLA Santa Monica Medical Center](https://health.usnews.com/best-hospitals/area/ca/ucla-medical-center-santa-monica-6933460). He received his medical degree from New York University Grossman School of Medicine and has been in practice for more than 20 years. Dr. David S. Liebeskind has expertise in treating stroke, among other conditions - see all areas of [expertise](https://health.usnews.com/doctors/david-liebeskind-447363#expertise). Dr. David S. Liebeskind accepts Medicare, Aetna, Blue Cross, United Healthcare - see other [insurance plans accepted](https://health.usnews.com/doctors/david-liebeskind-447363#insurance). Dr. David S. Liebeskind is highly recommended by patients.

## Specialties

Specialty

### Neurology

Neurologists diagnose and treat brain and nervous system disorders. Examples of medical conditions they treat include migraines, strokes, epilepsy, multiple sclerosis and sleep disorders, such as narcolepsy.

### What are Dr. Liebeskind's  areas of care?

Neurologists diagnose and treat brain and nervous system disorders. Examples of medical conditions they treat include migraines, strokes, epilepsy, multiple sclerosis and sleep disorders, such as narcolepsy.
